### [43] [Compaction as Epistemic Failure: How Agentic LLM Tools Fabricate Confirmed Results from Killed Processes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13071)
*Hiroki Tamba*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: Claude Code 等代理型 LLM 编程工具将超时命令（exit code 143）的部分标准输出错误地记录为已确认结果，导致错误信息在跨会话和跨模型版本中传播且无法被重新验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 揭示代理型 LLM 编程工具在会话压缩机制中存在的一个关键失败模式：将观察（终端输出）与持久化（实际存储）混为一谈，导致错误信息作为 ground truth 被继承。

Method: 通过分析 Claude Code 在处理超时命令（exit code 143）时的行为，记录其将部分标准输出写入压缩摘要（compaction summaries）并作为后续会话的事实依据的过程。

Result: 发现了观察与持久化的混淆机制：终端中出现的输出被等同于写入持久化存储的信息，且该错误会在不同会话和模型版本间传播而不进行重新验证。

Conclusion: 该失败模式扩展了先前关于 LLM 自我评估不可靠性的研究，表明代理型工具在报告自身操作结果时存在类似的可靠性缺陷，对所有依赖代理会话连续性进行数据处理、科学计算或多步骤自动化的工作流具有直接影响。

Abstract: Agentic LLM coding tools compress long session histories into compaction summaries that subsequent sessions inherit as ground truth. This paper documents a failure mode in Claude Code where partial standard output from timed-out commands (exit code 143) is recorded in compaction summaries as confirmed results, propagating false positives across sessions and model versions without re-verification. The underlying mechanism is a conflation of observation and persistence, where information that appeared in the terminal is treated as equivalent to information written to durable storage. This finding extends the analysis of LLM self-evaluation failures reported in prior work on non-determinism in LLM-as-judge grading by showing that agentic tools exhibit analogous reliability deficits when reporting on their own operational outcomes. The failure has direct implications for any workflow that relies on agentic session continuity for data processing, scientific computation, or multi-step automation.

</details>


### [44] [Inference Economics of Enterprise Coding Agents: A Case Study of Cloud vs. On-Premise LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13080)
*Sheng-Wei Peng,Yi-Hsun Lin,Yi-Pei Lee*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文通过28天对照实验，比较了基于API的Claude Opus（通过Claude Code）和本地部署的量化开源模型GLM-5.1/5.2（通过Opencode，NVIDIA Blackwell上NVFP4量化）在单开发者生产环境下的表现。研究发现：提示缓存（99.3%命中率）将API实际成本降至每百万token \$0.57，低于本地部署的分摊成本；但本地配置的缺陷修复提交比（FCR）高达74.9%（API为45.9%），修复概率是API的2.6-4.9倍。在台湾市场参数和共享GPU分配下，本地部署节省40.1%总拥有成本（TCO），但带来显著的开发者体验损失（调试循环增多、提交节奏变慢）。混合路由网关存在成本-质量权衡，无法完全超越纯API基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主编码智能体使工程组织面临两难选择：基于API的旗舰模型（推理强但token成本高）与本地部署的量化开源模型（边际成本低、数据主权好但推理 fidelity 损失）。本研究旨在量化这一 trade-off，为实际部署决策提供实证依据。

Method: 单开发者非随机化纵向案例研究，连续两个28天周期：API组使用Claude Opus 4.7/4.8 + Claude Code，本地组使用GLM-5.1/5.2 + Opencode（NVFP4量化，NVIDIA Blackwell）。分析LLM遥测数据和Git历史，计算提示缓存命中率、实际成本、缺陷修复提交比（FCR）、Mantel-Haenszel优势比等指标，并在台湾市场参数下进行TCO建模。

Result: API组：提示缓存99.3%命中率，实际成本降至\$0.57/百万token（低于本地\$2.83），FCR为45.9%。本地组：FCR高达74.9%，修复概率是API的2.6-4.9倍（Mantel-Haenszel OR=3.61）。共享GPU分配下本地部署节省40.1% TCO，但专用预留成本比缓存API高43.8%。离线回放显示混合路由网关存在成本-质量权衡。

Conclusion: 提示缓存可极大降低API实际成本，使得API模型在成本上具有竞争力。本地部署在共享资源下可节省TCO，但代价是显著的开发者体验损失（更多调试循环、更慢提交节奏）。混合路由网关无法同时优化成本和质量，存在帕累托前沿。决策需在成本节约与开发者生产力之间权衡。

Abstract: Autonomous coding agents force engineering organizations to choose between API-based frontier models -- strong reasoning at high token cost -- and on-premise quantized open-weights models, which promise low-marginal-cost scaling and data sovereignty at some loss of reasoning fidelity. We study this trade-off through a single-developer, non-randomized longitudinal case study over two contiguous 28-day periods on a production monorepo: an API-based Claude Opus 4.7/4.8 configuration using Claude Code versus an on-premise GLM-5.1/5.2 configuration using Opencode, quantized to NVFP4, on NVIDIA Blackwell hardware. Analyzing LLM telemetry and Git history, we find that prompt caching (99.3% hit rate) cuts realized API cost by 88.6% to an effective \$0.57 per million tokens -- below even the \$2.83 amortized unit cost of the shared on-premise slice (a utilization-dependent inversion; total realized spend and total cost of ownership (TCO) are the robust quantities). At comparable gross code churn, the local configuration was associated with a far higher defect-repair burden: a Fix Commit Ratio (FCR) of 74.9% versus 45.9%, with the odds of a commit being a repair 2.6 to 4.9 times higher within every difficulty tier (Mantel-Haenszel OR = 3.61). Under Taiwan-market parameters and a symmetric labor model, on-premise deployment nonetheless saves 40.1% of true TCO under shared GPU allocation, whereas dedicated reservation costs 43.8% more than the cached API. Under shared allocation, the genuine penalty is not monetary but a measurable developer-experience burden -- timestamp indicators show more work trapped in debugging spirals and a slower commit cadence -- and an offline replay shows hybrid routing gateways trade defect rate for infrastructure savings along a cost-quality frontier rather than dominate the pure-API baseline.

</details>


### [45] [Self-Improving AI Coding Agents Through Accumulated Behavioral Rules: A Closed-Loop Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13091)
*Aditya Aggarwal,Nahid Farhady Ghalaty*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出一个闭环框架，通过将人工审阅反馈转化为持久的行为规则，使LLM编码代理能够跨会话积累经验，无需更新模型权重即可持续改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于LLM的编码代理在不同会话中重复相同的错误类型，因为它们缺乏保留人工审阅反馈修正结果的机制。

Method: 构建闭环框架：(1) 版本控制的指令文件中累积规则集；(2) 代码提交前执行的自我审查清单；(3) 自动化验证确保规则集完整性。每次接受的审阅评论均编码为持久行为规则，逐步扩展代理可自我检测的错误类别。

Result: 在35+服务微服务平台上，规则集从5条增长到18条行为规则、15+语言标准及15项自检清单。11次记录会话(代码生成、PR审查、事件调查、跨服务重构)显示：审阅工作从低级正确性转向设计级验证，被规则覆盖的错误类别复发率为0%，规则可跨异构代理接口迁移。

Conclusion: 该框架实现了无需权重更新的持久跨会话学习，能在生产代码库上运行，解决现有基准未测量的行为一致性维度。编码代理在每个审阅周期中积累人类协作者的工程智慧。

Abstract: LLM-based coding agents repeat the same classes of mistakes across sessions because they lack a mechanism to retain corrections from human review feedback. We present a closed-loop framework in which every accepted review comment is codified as a persistent behavioral rule, progressively expanding the set of error classes the agent can self-detect. The framework combines an accumulating rule set in a version-controlled instruction file, a self-review checklist executed before code submission, and automated validation that ensures rule set integrity as it grows. In deployment across a 35+ service microservices platform, the rule set grew from 5 to 18 behavioral rules, 15+ language-specific standards, and a 15-item self-review checklist, all derived from real review feedback. We present empirical results from 11 recorded working sessions spanning code generation, PR review, incident investigation, and cross service refactoring. We observe that accumulated rules shift review effort from low-level correctness toward design-level validation, achieve a measured 0% recurrence rate for ruled-against error classes, and transfer across heterogeneous agent interfaces. We compare our approach against related work in experiential LLM learning (Reflexion, ExpeL, Voyager) and automated code review (CodeReviewer, SWE-bench agents), showing that our framework achieves persistent cross-session learning without weight updates, operates on production codebases rather than synthetic benchmarks, and addresses an orthogonal dimension (behavioral consistency over time) that existing benchmarks do not measure. The result is a coding agent that improves with every review cycle, accumulating the engineering wisdom of its human collaborators without changing a single model weight.

</details>


### [46] [SemaDiff: Identifying Semantic-Changing Commits with Generated Code and Tests](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13111)
*Maha Ayub,Michael Konstantinou,Ahmed Khanfir,Nikolaos Tsantalis,Mike Papadakis*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出SemaDiff方法，通过基于行为分析（比较提交前后版本的测试执行结果）来识别纯语义保留提交。对于难以测试的受重构影响代码，SemaDiff利用大语言模型生成额外的调用方法作为测试目标，并自动生成测试用例。若所有生成测试在两个版本中产生相同结果，则判定为语义保留提交。在183个Java开源项目提交的数据集上，SemaDiff以76%的准确率区分语义保留与变更提交，语义变更提交检测精度达100%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法能准确检测重构提交，但无法确保提交是纯语义保留的（不夹杂任何行为变更修改），这限制了调试、故障定位、错误数据集构建、回滚分析和补丁反向移植等任务。

Method: 提出SemaDiff方法：1) 分析diff识别修改代码并提取调用它的未变更依赖代码；2) 利用大语言模型生成额外的调用类来测试修改代码；3) 自动为依赖代码生成测试用例；4) 在提交前后版本上执行相同测试，若所有测试输出一致则判定为语义保留提交。

Result: 在183个手工标注的Java开源项目提交数据集上，SemaDiff以约76%的准确率区分语义保留与变更提交，语义变更提交检测精度达100%。

Conclusion: SemaDiff能够有效识别纯语义保留提交，为软件仓库挖掘中需要纯净重构提交的任务提供了可靠支持。

Abstract: Distinguishing semantic-preserving commits from changing ones remains an open challenge in software repository mining. While existing approaches detect refactoring commits accurately, they cannot ensure that a commit is purely semantic-preserving, without any interleaving behaviour-changing modification. This limitation can impact several tasks, such as debugging, fault localisation, bug dataset construction, rollback analysis, and bug fixes backporting. To fill this gap, we propose SemaDiff, a novel approach for identifying semantic-preserving commits through behaviour-based analysis; comparison of similar test execution on pre- and post-commit versions. As code impacted by the refactoring is often hard to test and different accross both versions, we propose generating additional calling methods to that code, which serve as testing target. Given a commit, SemaDiff analyses the diff to identify modified code and extracts unchanged dependent code that calls it. It then generates an additional dependent class using a large language model to exercise the changed code in both versions, and automatically generates tests for the dependent code. This way, we obtain the same tests for the different code versions, enabling the behavioural-difference detection. The commit is classified as semantic-preserving only if all generated tests produce identical outcomes across the two versions. To evaluate SemaDiff, we construct and annotate manually a dataset of 183 commits, gathered from well-known open-source Java projects. The obtained results show that SemaDiff distinguishes accurately semantic-preserving from -- changing commits in about 76% of the cases, with a 100% precision in semantic-changing commit detection.

</details>


### [47] [Design-System-Aware Development with AI: Evaluating Productivity and Design Consistency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13156)
*Luciane Silva,Thayssa Rocha,Nicole Davila,Gustavo Pinto*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文报告了在巴西大型企业进行的控制实验，比较了手动开发、仅设计系统开发和设计系统感知的AI辅助开发三种方式。结果表明，AI辅助开发显著减少了交付时间（46.7%-69.4%），提高了任务完成度，降低了性能变异性，并减少了工作流摩擦。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管设计系统有助于标准化前端开发，但开发者在将高保真模型转换为一致、可生产的界面时仍面临挑战。AI辅助工具虽已出现，但在设计系统为中心的工作流程中的有效性缺乏实证证据。

Method: 在巴西大型企业中进行控制实验，比较手动开发、仅设计系统开发和设计系统感知的AI辅助开发三种方式，涵盖Angular、iOS和Android技术栈，共两个实验周期。

Result: AI辅助开发减少了46.7%至69.4%的交付时间，提高了任务完成度，降低了性能变异性；模式分析表明工作流摩擦减少，任务执行更顺畅。

Conclusion: 设计系统感知的AI工具能够显著加速开发、提高设计保真度，为工业前端工作流带来实际效益。

Abstract: Design Systems (DS) help standardize front-end development, yet developers still face challenges when translating high-fidelity mockups into consistent, production-ready interfaces. Although AI-assisted tools have emerged as a potential solution, empirical evidence on their effectiveness within DS-centered workflows remains limited. This paper reports a controlled experiment conducted at a large Brazilian enterprise that compares manual development, DS-only development, and DS-aware AI-assisted development across Angular, iOS, and Android stacks. Results from two experimental cycles show that AI assistance significantly reduced time-to-delivery (by 46.7% to 69.4%), increased task completeness, and decreased performance variability. Analysis of break patterns further suggests reduced workflow friction and smoother task execution. These findings provide empirical evidence that DS-aware AI tools can significantly accelerate development, improve design fidelity, and yield practical benefits for industrial front-end workflows.

</details>


### [48] [From Human-Centric to Agentic Code Review: The Impact of Different Generations of Generative AI Technology on Review Quality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13196)
*Suzhen Zhong,Shayan Noei,Bram Adams,Ying Zou*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本研究分析了 GitHub 上 207 个项目中的 102 万次代码审查，揭示了从人工审查到 LLM 辅助审查再到 AI 代理审查的过渡过程。研究发现，AI 代理参与的合作模式（尤其是 AI 代理发起的审查或多 AI 代理审查）能加快审查决策速度，但不会提升审查质量。人机协作模式是审查效率的最强解释因素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 代码审查在保障软件质量的同时也给人类审查者带来沉重负担。随着生成式 AI 融入软件开发，代码审查正从以人类为主转向 AI 辅助的审查过程，但目前缺乏关于这种转变如何影响审查效率和质量的实证证据。

Method: 收集了 207 个 GitHub 项目中 102 万次经过审查的拉取请求，识别了三种 AI 审查者采用实践（渐进式 AI 采用、快速 LLM 采用、快速 AI 代理采用），并将拉取请求审查讨论建模为审查者交互序列，以刻画人类、LLM 和 AI 代理审查者的协作方式。

Result: AI 代理参与的合作模式（尤其是 AI 代理发起的审查或多 AI 代理审查）与更快的审查决策相关，但这种效率提升并未转化为更好的审查质量。审查活动类型和拉取请求类型在各个时代仍然重要，而一旦 LLM 和 AI 代理审查者参与，人机协作模式成为审查效率的最强解释因素。

Conclusion: 这些发现为设计既能提高效率又不削弱审查质量的 AI 辅助代码审查过程提供了实证指导。

Abstract: Code review helps maintain software quality before code integration, but it also imposes a substantial workload on human reviewers. As generative artificial intelligence becomes part of software development, code review is shifting from a primarily human review process toward AI-supported review processes in which large language model (LLM) reviewers and AI agent reviewers participate alongside human reviewers. However, we still lack empirical evidence on how this transition affects review efficiency and review quality. In this paper, we study 1.02 million reviewed pull requests from 207 GitHub projects that transition across three code review eras: human-centric review, LLM-assisted review, and agentic code review. We identify three AI reviewer adoption practices: Gradual AI Adoption, Rapid LLM Adoption, and Rapid AI Agent Adoption. We further model pull request review discussions as reviewer interaction sequences to characterize how human, LLM, and AI agent reviewers collaborate during the review process. Our results show that agent-involved collaboration patterns, especially reviews initiated by AI agents or involving multiple AI agents, are associated with faster review decisions under Gradual AI Adoption and Rapid AI Agent Adoption. However, these efficiency gains do not translate into better review quality. We also find that review activity and pull request type remain important across eras, while human-AI collaboration patterns become the strongest explanatory factor for review efficiency once LLM and AI agent reviewers participate. These findings provide empirical guidance for designing AI-supported code review processes that improve efficiency without weakening review quality.

</details>


### [49] [Can LLMs Learn and Apply Multi-Level Modelling Semantics? A First Empirical Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13257)
*Yuhong Fu,Weixing Zhang,Bowen Jiang,Haowei Cheng,Karamjti Kaur,Markus Stumptner*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文首次实证研究了大型语言模型（GPT-5.4、Claude Opus 4.6、Gemini 3.1 Pro）在多级建模（MLM）任务上的表现。在MULTI Warehouse Challenge基准上生成90个模型，发现语法正确性尚可，但语义正确性仅部分实现（实例化/特化正确性52%-79%），模型能复现显式内容但难以推断隐含结构。Claude表现最均衡，提示策略在精度与完整性间存在权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业5.0强调以人为本的工业系统设计，对建模工具提出更高要求。多级建模（MLM）能直接表示三个及以上抽象层级，但其复杂的语义约束使模型正确性难以保障。虽然LLM在模型驱动工程中已被广泛研究，但现有证据完全基于两层建模任务，而MLM的语义在种类上有所不同，其泛化能力尚未得到检验。因此，需要填补这一空白。

Method: 选取三种商业LLM（GPT-5.4、Claude Opus 4.6、Gemini 3.1 Pro），使用SLICER语言为MULTI Warehouse Challenge生成多级模型。在六种提示策略下共生成90个模型，并与手动验证的参考模型进行对比，使用14个指标进行评估。

Result: 语法正确性可达，但语义正确性仅部分实现：实例化/特化正确性52%-79%。模型能复现任务文本中明确陈述的内容，但很少补全文本隐含但未陈述的结构和约束。提示策略在精度与完整性间存在权衡。自我检查功能主要作为规则检查器，未能可靠改善与参考设计的一致性。Claude在三者中表现最均衡。

Conclusion: 研究结果明确了当前LLM在多级建模任务上的能力边界，为工业5.0背景下以人为中心、AI辅助建模工作流的设计提供了参考依据。

Abstract: Industry 5.0 emphasises human-centric industrial system design, placing additional demands on modelling tools. Multi-level modelling (MLM) can directly represent three or more abstraction levels, but this comes at the cost of more complex semantic constraints that model correctness depends on. Large Language Models (LLMs) have been increasingly studied in model-driven engineering, but this evidence rests entirely on two-level modelling tasks, and whether it generalises to MLM, whose semantics differ in kind, remains untested. This paper presents the first empirical study of this question. We have three commercial LLMs (GPT-5.4, Claude Opus 4.6, and Gemini 3.1 Pro) generate multi-level models for the MULTI Warehouse Challenge in the SLICER language under six prompting strategies, yielding 90 generated models compared against a manually validated reference using fourteen metrics. Syntactic correctness is within reach, but semantic correctness is only partially achieved, with Instantiation/Specialisation Correctness ranging from 52% to 79%. Models reproduce content stated explicitly in the task text, but rarely complete structure and constraints the text implies without stating. Prompting strategies trade off precision against completeness, and self-checking functions mainly as a rule checker rather than reliably improving alignment with the reference design. Among the three LLMs, Claude shows the most balanced profile. These results clarify the boundaries of current LLM capability for MLM and inform the design of human-centred, AI-assisted modelling workflows for Industry 5.0.

</details>


### [50] [Faithful Autoformalization of Natural Language Assertions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13303)
*Hongyi Liu,Madhusudan Parthasarathy,Adithya Murali*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出Monty框架，利用LLM从自然语言规范自动生成可执行断言，通过一致性评分和有效性测试过滤，在541个Java集合类断言生成任务中将精度平均提升20个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 正式合约对软件测试和验证至关重要，但手动编写断言既费力又易出错；LLM虽有自动形式化潜力，但面临断言有效性期望和自然语言歧义性的挑战。

Method: 提出Monty框架，核心包括：(1) 基于新颖的一致性评分指标过滤形式化结果；(2) 通过对代码进行断言测试获取有效性评分，双重过滤以提高断言质量。

Result: 在22个集合类Java类的541个断言生成任务上，相比直接用LLM翻译，该方法在精度上平均提升20个百分点，更可靠地生成真实断言。

Conclusion: Monty通过结合一致性评分和测试验证有效提升了LLM自动形式化断言的可靠性，验证了双重过滤机制在提升断言质量方面的有效性。

Abstract: Formal contracts are essential for software testing and verification, yet writing them remains labor-intensive and error-prone. LLMs offer a promising path toward autoformalization: synthesizing executable assertions from natural-language specifications and thereby bridging the gap between informal developer intent and formal executable specifications. We present Monty: an autoformalization framework for assertions that tackles the challenges of expectations of validity of assertions and ambiguity in natural-language. Our techniques are based on filtering formalizations using a novel conformance score metric and validity scores obtained from testing the code against formalized assertions. We evaluate our approach on 541 assertion-generation tasks derived from 22 collection-like Java classes, and show that our technique produces the ground truth more reliably (improving upto 20 points in precision on average) than when using LLMs naively to translate assertions.

</details>


### [51] [A Telemetry-Driven Model for Quantifying Upgrade Risk in Durable Workflow Execution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13617)
*Luca Maraschi,Matteo Collina*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种无需预演、沙盒或影子执行的闭式概率模型，用于量化工作流引擎中运行实例从旧版本升级到新版本的风险。该模型通过静态结构差异和已有遥测数据（事件日志、步骤负载、历史路径）计算风险，沿协议、接口、状态迁移三个维度分解，并利用贝叶斯估计和马尔可夫模型给出可信区间。同时，通过耦合图捕获运行间依赖，将机群风险建模为失败传播算子的最小不动点，并通过最小s-t割精确求解迁移/固定分区。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工作流引擎通过确定性重放不可变事件日志来恢复执行状态，这导致运行实例与产生其历史的代码版本紧密耦合：新部署会使旧版本启动的运行实例重放失效，导致状态静默损坏或执行停滞。现有缓解方案（固定版本、补丁门控、并排部署）将每次变更视为最大危险并排空旧版本，这对于休眠数周的工作流而言不可持续。因此需要一种能够量化升级风险并支持运行中实例平滑迁移的方法。

Method: 提出闭式概率模型，利用静态结构差异和已有遥测数据（事件日志、步骤负载、历史路径）计算升级风险，无需预演、沙盒或影子执行。风险沿三个维度分解：（1）协议层面，（2）接口层面，（3）状态迁移层面。模型结合精确的向后（重水合）项（基于记录前缀在并发完成下的轨迹等价类计算）和概率向前项（基于经验估计的马尔可夫控制流模型中的命中概率）。全程采用贝叶斯估计，使风险评分携带可信区间。通过耦合图捕获运行间依赖（钩子、层次结构、共享资源），机群风险建模为失败传播算子的最小不动点，耦合感知的迁移/固定分区通过最小s-t割精确求解。

Result: 证明向后风险为零的判定可确保在新版本下安全重水合，并推导出将运行实例分为迁移、审查和固定三类的策略。耦合感知的迁移/固定分区可通过最小s-t割精确计算。

Conclusion: 该方法提供了一种系统化的方式量化工作流升级风险，克服了现有方案在处理长时间运行工作流时的不足，既保证了安全又避免了不必要的版本排空开销。

Abstract: Durable workflow engines reconstruct execution state by deterministically replaying an immutable event log, coupling every in-flight run to the code version that produced its history: a new deployment can invalidate the replay of runs started under the old version, silently corrupting state or halting progress. Existing mitigations -- pinning, patch gates, side-by-side deployment -- treat every change as maximally dangerous and drain old versions, untenable for workflows that sleep for weeks.
  We present a closed-form probabilistic model that quantifies the risk of upgrading in-flight runs from workflow version $V_1$ to $V_2$ using only a static structural diff and telemetry the protocol already persists -- event logs, step payloads, historical paths -- with no dry-run, sandbox, or shadow execution. Risk decomposes along three axes (protocol, interface, state migration) and combines an exact backward (rehydration) term, computed on recorded prefixes modulo trace equivalence of concurrent completions, with a probabilistic forward term from hitting probabilities in an empirically estimated Markov model of control flow. Estimation is Bayesian throughout, so the Workflow Upgrade Risk (WUR) score carries a credible interval and thin telemetry surfaces as uncertainty. We prove that a zero backward-risk verdict certifies safe rehydration under the new version, and derive a policy partitioning runs into migrate, review, and pin classes. Finally we drop the inter-run independence assumption: coupling through hooks, hierarchy, and shared resources is captured by an empirical coupling graph, fleet risk becomes the least fixpoint of a failure-contagion operator, and the coupling-aware migrate/pin partition is computed exactly as a minimum s-t cut.

</details>


### [52] [The Replication Assessment Problem in Software Engineering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13815)
*Giuseppe Destefanis,Martin Shepperd,Leila Yousefi*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该论文通过对2021-2025年间软件工程重复性研究的系统综述，揭示了当前评估实践中存在的标准异质性、逻辑矛盾及测量不确定性缺失等问题，并提出了一套基于统计、方法和测量考量的原则性评估框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软件工程中的重复性研究日益增多，但由于评估标准松散且不一致，导致对重复结果的解读充满不确定性。本研究的动机在于系统记录当前评估实践中的问题，并推动建立一套可共享、可比较的评估标准。

Method: 作者对2021-2025年间实证软件工程领域的重复性研究进行了系统文献综述，提取各项研究中用于评估重复结果的标准，并从异质性、逻辑一致性以及与统计原则的贴合度三个维度进行分析。

Result: 共检索到10项重复性研究。分析发现：评估实践存在显著异质性，同类数据上出现了相互矛盾的标准；测量不确定性普遍未被充分重视；缺乏共享评估标准。作者据此提出了一个涵盖统计、方法和测量考量原则性框架，并通过示例进行了展示。

Conclusion: 采用一致且透明的评估原则能够减少歧义、提高研究间的可比性，从而支持软件工程重复性研究中更可靠的证据积累。

Abstract: Background: Replication studies in software engineering are increasingly common, yet their interpretation remains uncertain and inconsistent because assessments frequently rely on loosely defined or ad hoc criteria. Aim: This study aims to document how replication study outcomes are currently assessed in empirical software engineering, identify problems arising from inconsistent criteria and propose a principled framework for meaningful evaluation. Method: We conducted a systematic review of replication studies, with the search covering recent empirical software engineering replications (2021--2025). For each study, we extracted the criteria used to assess replication outcomes and analysed these for heterogeneity, logical consistency, and alignment with established statistical principles. Results: A total of 10 replication studies were located. The analysis reveals substantial heterogeneity in assessment practices, with contradictory criteria applied to similar data, limited acknowledgement of measurement uncertainty, and an absence of shared standards. We propose a principled framework grounded in statistical, methodological, and measurement considerations, and demonstrate its application through worked examples. Conclusions: Adopting consistent and transparent assessment principles would reduce ambiguity, improve comparability, and support more reliable evidence accumulation in software engineering replication research.

</details>


### [53] [PROBE: Benchmarking Code Generation in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13820)
*Rodrigo Pato Nogueira,Marco Vieira,João R. Campos*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出PROBE基准框架，从功能正确性、解决方案接近度和代码质量三个维度系统评估LLM代码生成能力，涵盖5种编程语言和多种提示策略，揭示LLM在难题、低资源语言上表现不佳，且常犯基础错误。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有代码生成基准局限于单一编程语言，过度依赖单元测试结果，忽略了生成代码的整体质量及其与有效解决方案的接近程度等关键维度，缺乏系统性、多维度的评估框架。

Method: 构建PROBE可扩展基准框架，包含多样化指标、代表性工作负载、多种提示模板和鲁棒的实验流程；从功能正确性、解决方案接近度、代码质量三个维度进行评估；对4个开源和2个闭源模型在5种编程语言、3种提示策略下进行实验，并辅以常见错误分析。

Result: LLM在代码生成上取得了一定成果，但在难题上表现较差；小型模型在训练资源较少的编程语言上表现不佳；模型常因基础性、可避免的错误而失败，凸显了自动生成代码的不可靠性。

Conclusion: 现有LLM在代码生成任务中仍存在明显局限，尤其在复杂问题和低资源语言上，且因基础性错误导致不可靠，需要更系统的评估框架来全面理解其能力与不足。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are increasingly being used in everyday software engineering tasks, particularly in automated code generation. Despite their widespread adoption, these models remain far from perfect, making systematic and fair evaluation essential to understand their strengths and limitations. In the context of code generation, existing benchmarks are limited: they often target a single programming language and rely primarily on unit test outcomes, while overlooking other critical dimensions such as the overall quality of the generated code and its closeness to a valid solution. To address these gaps, we introduce PROBE, an extensible benchmark framework that, unlike prior work, establishes a systematic structure built on diverse and well-defined metrics, representative workloads, varied prompt templates, and a robust experimental procedure. In practice, the code generated by the LLMs is evaluated along three complementary dimensions: functional correctness, proximity to valid solutions, and code quality, enabling a comprehensive assessment of performance. We use PROBE to evaluate four open-source and two proprietary models under three prompting strategies across five programming languages. We further complement this analysis with a study of common errors in the code and provide concrete examples, offering clearer insight into where LLMs tend to struggle. Our findings show that, while LLMs achieve promising results, they struggle with harder problems and, in the case of smaller models, with programming languages that have fewer available resources for training, and they often fail due to fundamental and easily avoidable errors that underscore the unreliability of automatically generated code.

</details>


### [54] [ProfMalPlus: Agent-Coordinated Detection of Malicious NPM Packages via Static-Dynamic Analysis Synergy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13965)
*Yiheng Huang,Zhijia Zhao,Bihuan Chen,Susheng Wu,Zhuotong Zhou,Yiheng Cao,Kun Hu,Xin Hu,Xin Peng*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: ProfMalPlus是一个结合对象敏感行为图与LLM推理的恶意NPM包检测器，通过静态分析、第三方信息增强和动态沙箱执行的多阶段协同机制，实现了98.1%的F1分数，并发现597个此前未知的恶意包。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开源软件易受通过传递依赖的供应链攻击，尤其是NPM包中注入的恶意代码。现有检测器存在诸多不足：未能充分建模混淆行为、忽视JavaScript的面向对象特性、静态与动态分析协同不佳、行为抽象过程中丢失语义信息。

Method: 1) 构建对象敏感行为图捕获敏感API、第三方调用和未解析调用；2) 提取安全相关切片并添加内联静态分析证据；3) 本地评判Agent独立评估各切片，通过自一致性合并重复评判降低LLM方差；4) 全局评判Agent综合生成入口级判定；5) 对未确定案例通过路由器选择第三方增强（注册表模块/方法语义）或动态增强（沙箱执行解析运行时依赖）；6) 定位Agent报告恶意代码片段及解释。

Result: ProfMalPlus实现98.1%的F1分数，比最先进的检测器高出3.5%至52.6%；共识别出597个此前未知的恶意包，均已被确认并从NPM移除。

Conclusion: 对象敏感行为图与LLM推理的有机结合能够有效弥补现有检测技术的不足，多阶段协同机制（静态分析→LLM评估→第三方/动态增强→二次评判）显著提升了恶意NPM包检测的准确性和覆盖面。

Abstract: Open source software is vulnerable to supply-chain attacks through transitive dependencies, especially malicious code injected into NPM packages. Existing detectors often inadequately model obfuscated behavior, overlook JavaScript's object-centric features, poorly coordinate static and dynamic analysis, and lose semantic information during behavior abstraction. We propose ProfMalPlus, a malicious NPM package detector combining object-sensitive behavior graphs with coordinated LLM reasoning over annotated code slices. It identifies installation commands and entry files, then constructs graphs capturing sensitive APIs, third-party calls, and unresolved calls. From these graphs, ProfMalPlus extracts security-relevant slices and adds inline static analysis evidence. Local judge agents independently assess each slice. Self-consistency consolidates repeated judgements to reduce LLM variance, while a global judge synthesizes their reports into an entry-level verdict. For undetermined cases, a router selects either third-party enrichment, which adds registry derived module and method semantics, or dynamic augmentation, which executes the package in a sandbox to resolve runtime dependent behavior. The enriched evidence is fed back for reassessment. Finally, a localization agent reports malicious code snippets with explanations. ProfMalPlus achieves a 98.1% F1-score, outperforming state-of-the-art detectors by 3.5% to 52.6%. It also identified 597 previously unknown malicious packages, all confirmed and removed from NPM.

</details>


### [55] [Early Adoption of Agentic Coding Tools by GitHub Projects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14037)
*Maliha Noushin Raida,Daqing Hou*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文分析了2,361个热门GitHub仓库中的25,264个智能体PR，发现智能体编码工具的采用集中在小部分项目中，小型项目参与度更高，人类-智能体协作以单人审查模式为主，多数项目的智能体PR生产率低于行业报告阈值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究多关注智能体生成PR的结果层面，但对项目层面如何采纳和管理智能体编码工具知之甚少，缺乏对开源项目中人类-智能体协作组织模式的实证理解。

Method: 收集2,361个热门GitHub仓库的25,264个智能体PR，从采用情况、项目级生产率、人类-智能体协作模式三个维度进行定量分析。

Result: （1）中位数仓库三个月仅产生1-2个智能体PR，密集采用集中在少数项目；（2）小型项目（1-5名贡献者）的参与比例和平均智能体PR活动水平高于中大型项目；（3）多数项目智能体PR生产率低于36 PR/参与者的行业估计；（4）人类-智能体协作以单人审查模式为主，多人协作模式不常见。

Conclusion: 智能体生成贡献的成功整合不仅取决于智能体能力进步，还取决于治理其使用的人类和组织流程。本研究提供了早期实证证据，未来需持续跟踪采用模式的演变。

Abstract: Agentic coding tools are increasingly capable of generating and submitting pull requests (PRs) to software projects, introducing new forms of human-agent collaboration in software development. While prior studies have examined PR-level outcomes of agent-generated contributions, less is known about how agentic coding tools are adopted and managed at the project level. In this paper, we analyze 25,264 agentic PRs from 2,361 popular GitHub repositories to investigate (1) the adoption of agentic coding tools, (2) project-level agentic PR productivity, and (3) human-agent collaboration patterns. Our results show that the median repository generates only one to two agentic PRs during a three-month period, indicating that intensive adoption remains concentrated in a small subset of projects. At the same time, small projects (1-5 contributors) exhibit higher participation ratios and average levels of agentic PR activity than medium-sized and large projects. We also observe substantial variation in project-level agentic PR productivity. While a small number of projects exceed an industry-reported estimate of 36 PRs per participant during the three-month observation period, most projects remain below this threshold. Finally, human-agent collaboration is dominated by a single-human oversight model, in which one developer reviews and/or modifies the agent's contributions, while multi-human collaboration patterns remain uncommon. These findings provide early empirical evidence on how open-source projects organize human oversight around agentic coding tools and suggest that successful integration of agent-generated contributions depends not only on advances in agent capabilities but also on the human and organizational processes that govern their use. Because this study captures an early snapshot of agent adoption, future work should continue to track how adoption patterns evolve over time.

</details>


### [56] [VisualRepair: Dynamic Tool Calling and Region Focusing for Visual Software Issue Repair](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14075)
*Jingyu Xiao,Zhongyi Zhang,Haoran Hou,Yuxuan Wan,Yuan Jiang,Yintong Huo,Michael R. Lyu*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: VisualRepair是一个基于MLLM的视觉软件问题修复框架，通过图像类型感知工具调用（ITTC）和动态测试时区域聚焦（DTRF）两个核心模块，有效利用bug截图中的视觉信息进行自动化程序修复。在SWE-bench Multimodal基准上，VisualRepair在测试集和开发集上分别解决了196和25个实例，超过最佳基线10和11个实例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动化程序修复方法在处理包含丰富图形用户界面的现代软件系统时，未能有效利用bug截图中的视觉信息。真实世界的问题报告包含多种异构视觉附件（如UI截图、IDE快照、GIF和文本图像），这些图像具有不同的视觉模式和领域特定语义，对多模态大语言模型（MLLM）造成巨大的感知负担。此外，bug截图中包含大量与bug无关的信息区域，会分散模型注意力并限制补丁多样性。

Method: VisualRepair包含两个核心模块：1) 图像类型感知工具调用（ITTC），对输入图像进行分类并动态调用定制化的工具调用链以实现鲁棒的视觉解读；2) 动态测试时区域聚焦（DTRF），定位多个与bug相关的候选区域，并通过自适应缩放（zoom-in和zoom-out）策略进行优化，以改进故障定位并促进多样化补丁生成。

Result: 在SWE-bench Multimodal基准上，VisualRepair在测试集上解决了196个实例，在开发集上解决了25个实例，分别超过最佳基线10个和11个实例。

Conclusion: VisualRepair在视觉软件问题修复任务中显著优于现有最先进方法，验证了类型感知的视觉理解和区域聚焦定位对于自动化视觉软件问题修复的有效性。

Abstract: Automated Program Repair (APR) has witnessed significant progress with the advent of Large Language Models (LLMs). However, as modern software systems increasingly expose rich graphical user interfaces, effectively leveraging visual information from bug screenshots has become essential for understanding bugs and generating accurate fixes in multimodal scenarios. Real-world issue reports frequently contain heterogeneous visual attachments including UI screenshots, IDE snapshots, GIFs, and text-centric images, each with distinct visual patterns and domain-specific semantics that impose substantial perceptual demands on MLLMs. Furthermore, bug screenshots often contain large expanses of uninformative and bug-irrelevant regions, distracting the model's attention and limiting patch diversity. To address these challenges, we propose VisualRepair, an MLLM-based framework for visual software issue repair comprising two core modules: Image Type-aware Tool Calling (ITTC), which classifies input images and dynamically invokes a tailored tool-calling chain for robust visual interpretation, and Dynamic Test-time Region Focusing (DTRF), which grounds multiple bug-related region candidates and refines them via an adaptive zoom-in and zoom-out strategy to improve fault localization and promote diverse patch generation. Extensive experiments on the SWE-bench Multimodal benchmark demonstrate that VisualRepair consistently outperforms state-of-the-art approaches. VisualRepair resolves 196 and 25 instances on the test and dev sets, respectively, surpassing the best baseline by 10 and 11 instances. These results highlight the effectiveness of type-aware visual understanding and region-focused localization for automated visual software issue repair.

</details>

### [57] [Automatic Differentiation from Scratch: How PyTorch Computes Gradients in Physics-Informed Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13042)
*Abdeladhim Tahimi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过1-3-3-1多层感知机和简单初值问题的完整数值追踪，详细展示了PyTorch自动微分引擎在PINN训练中如何计算物理导数（网络输出对输入的微分）和参数梯度（损失对网络参数的微分），并验证了所有伴随值与手推结果的对应关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文献通常抽象地描述PINN训练中的自动微分过程，缺乏对具体数值的逐节点追踪，导致研究者难以直观理解create_graph=True机制下的图上图（graph-on-graph）工作原理以及伴随值（adjoint values）与向量-雅可比积（VJP）之间的对应关系。

Method: 选取1-3-3-1结构的MLP和初值问题y'(t)+y(t)=0, y(0)=1作为测试用例，逐节点追踪前向传播构建的计算图、反向模式后向遍历计算全部22个参数梯度的过程，以及create_graph=True实现物理残差正确微分的图上图机制，并将每个伴随值与Tahimi (2026)的手工推导进行数值对照验证。

Result: 成功在数值层面完整还原了PINN训练中双层微分的全流程：前向计算图构建→物理导数求解→损失计算→反向传播求参数梯度，验证了create_graph=True如何通过在前向传播中额外构建用于物理残差微分的计算子图来实现梯度传播，所有22个参数梯度均与手工推导结果一致。

Conclusion: 本文的数值追踪方法为理解PyTorch autograd在PINN场景下的底层机制提供了直观且可复现的参考，将P/Q敏感性框架与向量-雅可比积的计算范式连接起来，有助于研究者更有效地使用自动微分进行科学计算。

Abstract: This paper traces, with explicit numerical values, how PyTorch's automatic differentiation (AD) engine computes gradients for Physics-Informed Neural Network (PINN) training -- a setting that requires two levels of differentiation: computing the physics derivative $\hat{y}'(t)=d\hat{y}/dt$ through the network, and computing parameter gradients $\nabla_θL$ of a loss that itself depends on $\hat{y}'(t)$. Using a 1-3-3-1 multilayer perceptron and the initial value problem $y'(t)+y(t)=0$, $y(0)=1$, we trace the complete pipeline at every node: the computational graph built during the forward pass, the reverse-mode backward traversal that computes all 22 parameter gradients in a single pass, and the graph-on-graph mechanism by which \texttt{create\_graph=True} enables correct differentiation through the physics-informed residual. Every adjoint value is verified against the hand derivations of Tahimi (2026), connecting the $P/Q$ sensitivity framework to the vector--Jacobian products used by PyTorch's autograd engine.

</details>


### [58] [Microstructure-Conditioned Surrogate Models for Graded Multiscale Optimization of Mycelium Composites](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13688)
*J. Storm,I. B. C. M. Rocha,S. Schyck,K. Masania,F. P. van der Meer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于超网络的混合物理-数据代理模型，通过将代理条件化于微结构变量，在少量训练数据下实现对菌丝-木屑复合材料多尺度力学行为的准确预测，并成功将功能梯度盘峰值应力降低42%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可持续材料依赖工程化层级和微结构控制性能，但多尺度优化需要大量数据训练代理模型，尤其是当考虑微结构变化时数据需求更大。现有方法难以在数据有限的情况下高效建模多种微结构。

Method: 使用超网络（hypernetwork）将混合物理-数据代理模型条件化于微结构变量，实现对不同微结构的泛化；进一步将网络条件化于制造变量以直接关联工艺参数；采用FE²模拟验证。

Result: 在小数据集下实现对菌丝-木屑复合材料多尺度力学行为的准确预测；优化后的功能梯度盘相比随机微结构设计峰值应力降低42%。

Conclusion: 条件化代理模型使微结构的多尺度优化变得可行，为可持续材料的设计和开发提供了高效路径，加速了微观结构工程化的实际应用。

Abstract: Emerging sustainable materials increasingly rely on engineered hierarchy and microstructure to achieve control of their properties and mechanical behavior. Optimizing these materials with controllable microstructures requires efficient multiscale simulations. Data-driven surrogate models for the microscale can accelerate multiscale simulations, but require large amounts of data even for a fixed microstructure. When a range of microstructures is considered, as is the case in multiscale optimization, even more data is needed to train a surrogate. To overcome this challenge, we condition a hybrid physics-data surrogate on microstructural variables using a hypernetwork. This approach enables accurate predictions of multiscale mechanical behavior for a mycelium-woodchip composite material, even when trained on small datasets. The conditioned surrogate makes multiscale simulations of functionally graded structures tractable, and we validate it against a full FE^2 simulation. We optimize a graded multiscale disk, and reduce the peak stress by 42% compared to one with a random microstructure. Then, we go one step further, conditioning the network directly on manufacturing variables that can have a complex influence on the microstructure. This is a practical route to engineer the microscale for desired macroscale behavior. This contribution highlights the benefits of microarchitectured structures and demonstrates how conditioned surrogate models enable their multiscale optimization, which will accelerate the development and design of future sustainable materials and structures.

</details>


### [59] [Beyond Backbone Backpropagation: A Decoupled Strategy for Efficient Transfer Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13043)
*Daniel Vila-Cruz,Laura Morán-Fernández,Verónica Bolón-Canedo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种轻量级训练策略，通过解耦特征提取与分类器优化、适配归一化层及改进分类器头部，在多种CNN和Transformer架构及三个医学数据集上显著降低训练时间和碳排放，仅牺牲极小的准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习模型在图像分类中性能优异，但计算成本和能耗高，阻碍了在资源受限环境（如临床或原型开发）中的部署，亟需一种高效且环境可持续的训练方法。

Method: 1) 轻量级训练策略，适配模型的归一化层到新领域；2) 解耦特征提取与分类器优化，仅一次性预计算特征以减少开销；3) 重新设计分类器头部，引入基于边际的加权损失，无需端到端反向传播。

Result: 在四种CNN架构（ResNet18, ResNet50, MobileNet, DenseNet121）、三种Transformer模型（ViT, Swin, DeiT）和三个医学数据集（Brain Cancer MRI, BreakHis, PatchCamelyon）上，显著减少训练时间，准确率仅有微小损失，经常达到或超过基线性能，碳排放降低数个数量级。

Conclusion: 该方法为资源受限的临床或原型开发环境提供了高效且环境可持续的深度学习解决方案。

Abstract: Deep learning models achieve state-of-the-art image classification but face deployment challenges due to computational costs and energy demands. We propose a lightweight training strategy that adapts normalization layers of the model to the new domain and decouples feature extraction from classifier optimization, reducing overhead by precomputing features only once. A redesigned classifier head with margin-based weighted loss further minimizes ambiguity without end-to-end backpropagation. Evaluated across four CNN architectures (ResNet18, ResNet50, MobileNet, DenseNet121), three Transformer models (ViT, Swin and DeiT) and three medical datasets (Brain Cancer MRI, BreakHis and PatchCamelyon), our approach significantly reduces the required training time with only a marginal accuracy trade-off, often matching or surpassing baseline performance. This efficiency translates to reducing CO2 by orders of magnitude, offering a practical and environmentally sustainable solution for resource-constrained clinical or prototyping environments.

</details>


### [60] [Federated Explainable Artificial Intelligence: Roles, Architectures, Evaluation, and Open Challenges](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13045)
*Masoume Gholizade,Fabrizio Ruffini,Pietro Ducange,Francesco Marcelloni*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统综述了联邦可解释人工智能（FedXAI）领域，阐述了可解释性如何从事后工具演变为联邦学习全生命周期的核心组成部分，提出了基于可解释性角色、模型类型、解释范围、集成层次、FL设置和数据异质性等维度的分类体系，并指出了标准化基准缺失、非独立同分布数据下的可解释性、面向解释的安全威胁等关键挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习虽保护数据隐私，但无法解决模型黑箱问题；可解释AI虽提升透明度，但尚未与联邦学习充分融合。二者的交叉——联邦可解释人工智能（FedXAI）——旨在同时满足隐私与可解释性需求，但目前缺乏系统性梳理和统一框架。

Method: 采用系统性文献综述方法，对FedXAI现有工作进行分类与整合。提出多维度分类法：可解释性角色（事后工具 vs. 生命周期内置组件）、模型与解释器类型、解释范围（全局/局部）、集成层次、FL设置（横向/纵向/联邦迁移学习）及数据异质性。覆盖从模型不可知解释、可解释联邦模型到可解释性感知聚合机制的方法。

Result: 揭示了可解释性在联邦学习中的多重作用：支持聚合、个性化、鲁棒性、协调和系统级决策。指出现有评估缺乏标准化基准和统一指标（解释质量、稳定性、隐私泄露、计算开销）。总结关键挑战：非独立同分布数据下的可解释性、面向解释的安全威胁、通信高效XAI、持续FedXAI、领域知识与法规约束整合。

Conclusion: FedXAI是设计可信、透明且隐私保护的联邦AI系统的关键范式。可解释性需从事后工具转变为FL生命周期的主动组件。当前领域尚处于早期阶段，亟需标准化评估体系、应对非独立同分布数据的可解释性方法，以及兼顾通信效率与解释质量的实用方案。

Abstract: Federated Learning (FL) has emerged as a key paradigm for privacy-preserving collaborative model training across distributed and heterogeneous data sources. By keeping raw data local, FL addresses data confidentiality concerns, yet it does not resolve the opacity of modern machine learning models. In parallel, Explainable Artificial Intelligence (XAI) has gained attention for improving transparency, trust, and accountability, particularly in high-stakes domains. Their intersection has given rise to Federated Explainable Artificial Intelligence (FedXAI) paradigm, which aims to jointly satisfy privacy and explainability requirements. This survey provides a systematic review of FedXAI, highlighting the transition of explainability from a post-hoc tool to an integral component of the FL lifecycle. We show how explainability supports aggregation, personalization, robustness, coordination, and system-level decision making. To organize the literature, we introduce a taxonomy that classifies FedXAI methods by the role of explainability, model and explainer types, explanation scope, integration level, FL settings, and data heterogeneity. We review approaches ranging from model-agnostic explanations to interpretable federated models and explainability-aware aggregation mechanisms. We also examine evaluation practices and discuss the lack of standardized benchmarks and metrics for measuring explanation quality, stability, privacy leakage, and computational overhead. Finally, we identify key challenges, including explainability under non-IID data, explanation-centric security threats, communication-efficient XAI, continual FedXAI, and the integration of domain knowledge and regulatory constraints. By consolidating existing work and identifying key gaps, this survey serves as a reference framework for designing trustworthy, transparent, and privacy-preserving federated AI systems.

</details>


### [61] [What Your Model Threw Away and Why You'll Want It Back: Masking, Fingerprinting, and Privacy from Discarded Geometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13046)
*Zachary P. Bradshaw*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个用于分析机器学习模型中信息丢弃现象的框架，该模型输入携带李群作用。定义了零化纤维（null fiber）和稳定子群（stabilizer）两个关键概念，分别刻画了模型在群作用下的不变性。利用预像定理和Peter-Weyl定理给出了理论保证，并提出了基于牛顿迭代的高效计算方法。在分子性质预测和球面图像分类等任务上进行了实验验证，适用于经典神经网络和变分量子电路。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器学习模型在处理具有对称性的数据（如分子结构、球面图像）时，往往会在群作用下丢弃部分信息，但缺乏统一的理论框架来刻画这种信息丢失。作者希望建立一个严格的数学框架，量化模型在何种群作用下保持不变，并利用这种不变性实现数据掩码、模型指纹保护和隐私计算等应用。

Method: 1) 定义零化纤维 $N_G(f,x)$ 为在点 $x$ 处对 $f$ 不可检测的群元素集合；2) 定义稳定子群 $\mathrm{Stab}_G(f)$ 为 $f$ 在 $G$ 下的最大不变子群；3) 利用预像定理证明光滑映射的零化纤维在一般输入处维数至少为 $\dim G - 1$；4) 对紧致群作用于自身的情况，利用Peter-Weyl定理给出傅里叶系数矩阵的谱特征刻画；5) 提出基于牛顿迭代的轨道映射计算方法，计算复杂度与几次梯度评估相当。

Result: 在分子性质预测（SO(3)群）和球面图像分类（PSL(2,C)群）任务上验证了框架的有效性，展示了数据掩码、模型指纹和隐私保护等应用。该框架统一适用于经典神经网络和变分量子电路。

Conclusion: 本文建立了一个统一的理论框架来理解和利用机器学习模型在对称性下的信息丢弃行为，为空域上的信息压缩和隐私保护提供了理论基础和实用工具。

Abstract: We develop a framework for the information discarded by machine learning models whose inputs carry a Lie group action. Given a representation $π$ of a Lie group $G$ on a space $V$ and a learned function $f\colon V \to \mathbb{R}$, we define two objects measuring the symmetry invisible to $f$. The null fiber at a point $x \in V$ is the set $N_G(f,x) = \{g \in G : f(π(g^{-1}) \cdot x) = f(x)\}$ of group elements whose inverse action on $x$ is undetectable by $f$. When $N_G(f,x)$ is independent of $x$, it coincides with the stabilizer $\mathrm{Stab}_G(f)$, the largest subgroup of $G$ under which $f$ is invariant. For smooth maps to $\mathbb{R}$, the preimage theorem guarantees that null fibers have dimension at least $\dim G - 1$ at generic inputs, regardless of architecture. For compact groups acting on themselves, the Peter--Weyl theorem yields a spectral characterization of both objects in terms of the Fourier coefficient matrices of $f$. We show that null fiber elements can be computed efficiently via Newton iteration on the orbit map, at a cost comparable to a few gradient evaluations. Applications to data masking, model fingerprinting, and privacy-preserving computation are developed and tested experimentally on molecular property prediction under $\mathrm{SO}(3)$ and spherical image classification under the Möbius group $\mathrm{PSL}(2, \mathbb{C})$. The framework applies uniformly to classical neural networks and variational quantum circuits.

</details>


### [62] [Targeted Recovery of Weight-Space Mechanisms From Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13047)
*Antoine Vigouroux,Lee Sharkey*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出目标参数分解（tPD），通过引入高秩catch-all组件处理非目标数据，仅分解处理特定输入的组件，大幅降低计算成本，并在多个规模的Transformer模型上验证了其忠实性和可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有参数分解（PD）方法虽能将神经网络分解为忠实反映原始网络操作的可解释计算组件，但扩展到大规模模型时计算成本极高，限制了其实际应用。

Method: 提出目标参数分解（targeted PD, tPD），通过引入一个高秩catch-all组件处理所有非目标数据，使得仅需识别和处理特定输入（从孤立提示到大型子任务）的相关组件，大幅降低计算开销。

Result: 在玩具模型和基于The Pile训练的Transformer语言模型上验证了tPD的有效性；在4-block Transformer中仅用原公开分解7%的FLOPs即可提取CSS-only子模型；在12-block Transformer中成功消融和重写记忆序列，且对其他输入的副作用可忽略不计。

Conclusion: tPD能在大模型上以更低计算成本实现可复现、机制上忠实的电路分解，为大规模神经网络的可解释性研究提供了实用的技术路径。

Abstract: Parameter decomposition (PD) decomposes neural networks into interpretable computational components that faithfully reflect the original network's operations. However, scaling PD to large models requires vast compute, making it a costly and risky endeavor. Here we propose targeted PD (tPD), which identifies only the components that process specific inputs of interest -- from isolated prompts to large subtasks -- by introducing a high-rank catch-all component that handles all non-target data. We validate tPD on toy models and on transformer language models trained on The Pile, where it recovers reproducible, mechanistically faithful circuits. We extract a CSS-only submodel of a 4-block transformer using 7% of the FLOPs of its published decomposition, and in a 12-block transformer we surgically ablate and rewire memorized sequences, with negligible side effects on other inputs.

</details>


### [63] [Uncertainty-Aware Sequential Decision Rules for Event-Triggered LLM Invocation in Streaming Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13048)
*Zhaohui Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出将流式推理管线中何时调用大语言模型(LLM)的问题形式化为基于风险的序列停止问题，并证明了阈值策略的最优性、亚线性遗憾界等六个理论结果，在CMAPSS数据集上验证了理论假设并展示了约一个数量级的Pareto AUC优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流式推理管线中轻量模型与LLM的混合使用日益普遍，但"何时触发LLM"这一核心问题缺乏严格的理论处理。

Method: 将问题形式化为基于风险的序列停止问题：当观测历史的风险泛函超过阈值时触发策略。框架涵盖了事件触发、最优停止、SPRT、CUSUM和贝叶斯触发等经典触发策略族。

Result: 1）六个理论证明，包括最小事件间隔界、阈值策略最优性、SPRT近似保证、亚线性遗憾界等；2）CMAPSS数据集上实证验证理论假设；3）异常分数驱动的风险函数在Pareto AUC上优于其他方案约一个数量级；4）92.9%的LLM诊断达到grounding score≥0.75。

Conclusion: 所有原则性触发策略均达到α＜1的亚线性遗憾；异常分数驱动的风险函数具有显著优势；理论框架能统一多种经典触发策略。

Abstract: Streaming inference pipelines increasingly pair lightweight fast models with Large Language Models (LLMs) that provide rich semantic understanding at substantial cost. The central question of when to invoke the LLM has received limited formal treatment. We cast this as a risk-based sequential stopping problem, where a trigger policy fires when a risk functional over the observation history exceeds a threshold. Within this framework, we prove six results: a minimum inter-event time bound excluding trigger chattering; optimality of threshold policies via smooth pasting; approximate SPRT guarantees under estimated parameters; O(sqrt(T log T)) regret for stationary streams, extending to O(sqrt((C_T + 1) T log T)) under C_T changepoints; O(1/sqrt(T)) convergence of online gradient descent for adaptive thresholds; and a calibration-to-miss-rate transfer inequality. Several classical trigger families, including event-triggered, optimal stopping, SPRT, CUSUM, and Bayesian triggers, can be expressed as special cases of this framework. On turbofan degradation data (CMAPSS) with real LLM calls, we empirically verify the theoretical assumptions, ablate the risk function design, compare against six baselines including a RouteLLM-style router and contextual bandits, and analyze cost sensitivity and LLM failure modes. The results confirm sublinear regret, with alpha < 1 for all principled triggers; high diagnostic quality, with 92.9 percent of 1600 LLM diagnoses reaching grounding score >= 0.75 under our rubric; and that anomaly-score-driven risk functions dominate alternatives by roughly an order of magnitude on the Pareto AUC.

</details>


### [64] [Gauge-Invariant, Parameter-Insensitive Regularization for Potential Recovery from Flow on Directed Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13609)
*Mohammad Forouhesh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了有向图上的势恢复问题（离散泊松问题）存在的规范不变性缺陷：岭回归正则化会使解收缩到规范无意义的原点，导致排序反转（秩相关从+0.81降至-0.42）。提出用图Dirichlet能量（H¹半范数）作为规范不变的正则化项，消除该问题并实现参数不敏感性（λ跨四个数量级保持稳定）。理论证明简化后的求解器是SPD的，并能在岭回归失效处保持动态范围。在三个公开点击流数据集上，规范不变估计保留28-41%的内部动态范围，而岭回归仅保留0.2%。该规范不变性还可推广到图神经网络，每层消除常数模式可防止深层有向GCN的过平滑问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 有向图上从观测流恢复潜在势（带Dirichlet边界的离散泊松问题）是病态的，标准的岭回归正则化会向规范无意义的原点收缩，导致恢复的排序反转（+0.81→-0.42的秩相关）。需要一种规范不变的正则化方法来解决这一根本性问题。

Method: 提出使用图Dirichlet能量（即H¹半范数）作为规范不变的正则化项替代岭回归。理论证明该简化求解器是SPD的，能精确在岭回归失效处保持动态范围。通过泊松残差从流中局部化吸收边界。在三个公开点击流数据集上验证，并将该规范不变性扩展到图神经网络中每层消除常数模式。

Result: 规范不变估计跨四个数量级的λ保持参数不敏感；在三个点击流数据集上保留28-41%的内部动态范围，而岭回归仅保留0.2%；岭回归对每个λ>0都导致排序反转。该规范不变性还能防止深层有向GCN的过平滑问题。

Conclusion: 图Dirichlet能量（H¹半范数）作为规范不变正则化能有效解决有向图势恢复的病态问题，提供参数不敏感的稳定估计，并能推广到图神经网络中解决过平滑问题，将经典逆问题与图学习核心问题联系起来。

Abstract: Recovering a latent potential from observed flow on a directed graph (a discrete Poisson problem with Dirichlet boundaries) is ill-posed, and the standard fix backfires: ridge regularization shrinks toward a gauge-meaningless origin, collapsing and reversing the recovered ordering ($+0.81\to-0.42$ rank correlation against a planted ground truth). The gauge-invariant graph Dirichlet energy removes the hazard and delivers parameter-insensitivity: the estimate is stable across four orders of magnitude in $λ$, whereas ridge inverts the ordering for every $λ>0$. We prove the reduced solve is SPD and preserves dynamic range exactly where ridge collapses it, and localize absorbing boundaries from flow alone via a Poisson residual. The $H^1$ seminorm is classical; what is new is the gauge diagnosis, the parameter-insensitivity it buys, and an ablation showing the result is robust to the extraction method. On three public clickstream corpora the gauge-invariant estimate retains $28$--$41\%$ of the interior dynamic range while ridge collapses to as little as $0.2\%$. The same gauge invariance carries into graph neural networks -- neutralizing the constant mode per layer prevents the oversmoothing that collapses a deep directed GCN -- linking this classical inverse problem to a central question in graph learning.

</details>


### [65] [TSSM: Triaxial State Space Model for Global Station Weather Forecasting with Temporal-Variable-Historical Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13101)
*Songru Yang,Zili Liu,Tao Han,Ben Fei,Fenghua Ling,Lei Bai,Chang Liu,Xiangyang Ji,Zhenwei Shi,Zhengxia Zou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出三轴状态空间模型（TSSM），通过引入历史增强的时间-变量-历史（TVH）范式，将周期对齐的历史样本堆叠成批次进行扫描，解决了现有方法依赖短期模式导致的极端事件预测差和误差累积问题。TSSM在最大站点天气数据集Weather-5K上达到SOTA，准确率和极端事件指标分别提升10%和61%，在长时预测中最高提升103.5%，且在80%观测缺失下仍保持>90%性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有全球站点天气预报方法过度依赖短期时间窗口模式，难以捕捉混沌天气动力学特性，在极端事件预测和误差累积方面表现不佳，且部分观测条件下性能大幅下降。需要一种能有效整合长周期、大尺度历史信息的方法来突破这些限制。

Method: 提出Triaxial State Space Model (TSSM)，核心是TVH（Temporal-Variable-Historical）范式。将历史样本按周期对齐堆叠为批次，使预测同时获得历史与当前观测的因果支持。设计三个轴向扫描：时间轴（捕获时序依赖）、变量轴（捕获变量相关性）、历史轴（捕获历史演化）。通过层次共享结构建模从季节到极端事件的多尺度模式，并缓解历史模式间的错位问题。

Result: 在Weather-5K（最大站点天气数据集）上实现SOTA，精度提升10%，极端事件指标提升61%；在人类参与数据集上获得95%的最佳或次佳结果。长时预测（240h）提升37.5%，迭代预测（48h×5次）最高提升103.5%。在80%观测缺失下仍保持>90%性能，而基线方法低于43%。

Conclusion: TSSM通过引入历史增强的TVH三轴扫描范式，有效解决了短视依赖问题，在长时预测、极端事件捕获和部分观测鲁棒性方面显著优于现有方法，为全球站点天气预报提供了一种可靠且实用的解决方案。

Abstract: Global Station Weather Forecasting (GSWF) is pivotal for localized and extreme weather prediction over key regions. Despite efforts to exploit look-back windows, existing methods show limited accuracy gains and struggle with extreme events and error accumulation. These limitations stem from overreliance on short-term patterns, which are insufficient to capture chaotic weather dynamics, especially under partial observations. To address this problem, we propose a novel Triaxial State Space Model (TSSM) with a history-enhanced Temporal-VariableHistorical paradigm, which incorporates period-aligned historical weather data to compensate for long-term, large-scale periodic, and full-window weather patterns beyond the temporal lookback window. Specifically, TSSM stacks historical samples into period-aligned batches, where forecasting is causally supported by historical and current observations. Temporal, variable, and historical scanning are designed to capture axial temporal dependencies, variable correlations, and historical evolution. This structure is hierarchically shared to model seasonal to extreme events while alleviating misalignment across historical patterns. TSSM achieves SOTA performance on Weather-5K, the largest station weather dataset to date, with 10% and 61% gains in accuracy and extreme event metrics, and obtains 95% best or second-best results on human-involved datasets. Its advantages are more pronounced in long-horizon and iterative forecasting, reaching a 37.5% gain at 240h and up to 103.5% under a 48h times 5 iterative setting. Moreover, TSSM retains > 90% performance under up to 80% missing observations, compared with < 43% for baselines, demonstrating robustness and practical potential for reliable GSWF in global in-situ observation networks.

</details>


### [66] [Disentangling Knowledge States with Ability and Proficiency Modeling for Knowledge Tracing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13103)
*Duantengchuan Li,Yingqian Bi,Jinsong Chen,Rui Zhang,Mingwen Tong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种阶段感知的知识追踪框架PAKT，将学生学习行为分解为能力构建阶段和熟练度提升阶段，并设计多分支Transformer联合建模阶段特定与整体知识状态，在六个基准数据集上取得平均0.82%的AUC提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有知识追踪方法将学生的原始交互序列视为统一的单一行为过程，忽略了学习行为中存在的阶段性特征（如从能力构建到熟练度导向学习的转变），导致对复杂学习行为中混杂偏差的忽视。

Method: 提出PAKT框架：1）设计特定的分解机制，将学生交互序列划分为能力阶段和熟练度阶段；2）构建多分支Transformer结构，配合类型感知读出模块，联合捕获阶段特定知识状态与整体知识状态；3）提供因果分析以揭示阶段无关模型中混杂偏差的来源。

Result: 在六个公开基准数据集上，PAKT一致性地优于代表性基线方法，最大AUC提升1.33%，平均提升0.82%。

Conclusion: 显式建模学习行为的阶段性结构可以有效提升知识追踪的预测性能，因果分析进一步解释了阶段无关模型性能受限的内在原因。

Abstract: Knowledge tracing (KT) aims to predict students' future performance by modeling their evolving knowledge states from historical interactions. Existing KT methods usually treat the raw interaction sequence as a unified behavioral process, overlooking the phase-specific nature of learning behaviors. Our preliminary observations show that students are more likely to correctly answer previously failed knowledge concepts after sufficient practice, suggesting a transition from ability-building to proficiency-oriented learning. Motivated by this, we propose Phase-Aware Knowledge Tracing (PAKT), a KT framework that decomposes student interactions into ability and proficiency phases based on the tailored decomposition mechanism. To effectively exploit the decomposed sequences, we design a multi-branch Transformer with a type-aware readout module to jointly capture phase-specific and holistic knowledge states. We further provide a causal analysis to reveal the confounding bias caused by entangling complex learning behaviors in phase-agnostic KT models. Extensive experiments on six public benchmarks demonstrate that our method consistently outperforms representative baselines, with a maximum AUC gain of 1.33% and an average gain of 0.82%.

</details>


### [67] [STKAN: Kolmogorov-Arnold Networks for Spatio-Temporal Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13108)
*Sicong Lai,Yuehong Hu,Siru Zhong,Si Qiao,Yuxuan Liang,Guangyin Jin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出STKAN架构，通过引入泰勒多项式KAN模块改进时空预测中的非线性函数逼近能力，在五个交通预测基准上取得竞争性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有时空预测方法集中于图、注意力、分解等架构设计，但对底层非线性函数逼近器的影响关注不足，作者希望探索改进函数逼近器对时空预测性能的提升作用。

Method: 提出STKAN架构：1) 通过可学习的软节点组分配机制构建高层空间表示；2) 分组空间混合；3) 对压缩序列建模时间依赖；4) 额外使用空间和时间自注意力层捕获长程交互。核心是将泰勒多项式KAN模块引入空间和时间token混合中。

Result: 在五个交通预测基准上，STKAN取得竞争性能，且优于评估的MLP变体。

Conclusion: 非线性函数逼近器的设计可以作为时空预测中架构设计的有用补充。

Abstract: Real-world traffic data exhibit heterogeneous spatial correlations and nonlinear temporal dynamics, posing substantial challenges for accurate spatio-temporal forecasting. Existing approaches have developed increasingly sophisticated graph, attention, and decomposition architectures, while the influence of the underlying nonlinear function approximator has received comparatively less attention. In this work, we propose STKAN, a spatio-temporal forecasting architecture that introduces Taylor-polynomial Kolmogorov--Arnold Network modules into spatial and temporal token mixing. STKAN first constructs high-level spatial representations through a learnable soft node-group assignment mechanism, applies group-wise spatial mixing, and subsequently models temporal dependencies over the compressed sequence. Spatial and temporal self-attention layers are further employed to capture long-range interactions. Experiments on five traffic forecasting benchmarks show that STKAN achieves competitive performance and performs better than the evaluated MLP-based variant in the tested settings. These results suggest that the design of nonlinear function approximators can serve as a useful complement to architectural design in spatio-temporal forecasting.

</details>


### [68] [A Hybrid Mamba for Audio-Visual Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13110)
*Yi Wang,Yinfeng Yu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Samba模型，利用Mamba架构替代传统CNN/RNN，通过Mamba状态编码器(M-SE)和音频Mamba编码器(AME)分别改进时序聚合和频谱特征提取，在音频-视觉导航任务上显著超越现有SOTA模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自2020年以来，基于CNN和RNN的音频-视觉导航骨干网络已逾五年未有本质变化，难以高效表达动态多模态序列，亟需架构革新。

Method: 提出Samba模型：(1) 使用自适应选择机制的Mamba状态编码器(M-SE)替代传统GRU进行时序聚合；(2) 构建音频Mamba编码器(AME)克服卷积算子在捕捉频谱图全局时频依赖方面的局限性。

Result: 在Matterport3D数据集上导航成功率(SR)提升11.3%；在场景结构更精细的Replica数据集上性能增益更显著；展现出对未听过声源和未见场景的优异泛化能力。

Conclusion: Samba通过现代化的架构重建，以更低计算成本实现更强的具身表征能力，为音频-视觉导航领域的范式演进提供了高鲁棒性的技术路径。

Abstract: Since the paradigm centered on convolutional neural networks and recurrent architectures was established in 2020, the fundamental backbone networks for audio-visual navigation have undergone no essential changes for more than five years, making them inadequate to support efficient representation of dynamic multimodal sequences. This paper proposes Samba(A Hybrid Mamba for Audio-Visual Navigation). It uses the adaptive selection-enabled Mamba State Encoder (M-SE) to replace conventional GRUs for temporal aggregation, and constructs an Audio Mamba Encoder (AME) to remedy the limitations of convolutional operators in capturing global time-frequency dependencies in spectrograms. Experiments demonstrate that Samba exhibits exceptional generalization performance when facing unheard sound sources and unseen scenes. On the Matterport3D dataset, it improves the navigation success rate (SR) by 11.3\% compared with existing state-of-the-art models, and the performance gain is even more pronounced on the Replica dataset, which features finer scene structures. Such modernized architectural reconstruction unlocks stronger embodied representation capabilities at a lower computational cost, thereby providing a highly robust technical pathway for paradigm evolution in the field of audio-visual navigation.

</details>


### [69] [CoDiffGRN: Rethinking Gene Regulatory Network Inference via the BEELINE-KGC Benchmark and Co-evolutionary Discrete Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13120)
*Jiaze Song,Runhao Zhao,Minghao Xu,Bin Cui,Wentao Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出GRN推理应被重新定义为归纳式、以排序为中心的图补全问题，并引入新基准Benchmark和共进化离散扩散框架Method，在新型调控关系发现上显著超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基因调控网络推断方法存在根本性错配：研究者通常需要少量高置信度的调控关系（尤其涉及未见过的基因）用于实验验证，但当前基准依赖直推式划分和全局分类指标，且主流模型在归纳设置下泛化能力差。

Method: 1) 将GRN推断重新定义为归纳式、以排序为中心的图补全问题；2) 提出新基准Benchmark，包含归纳式基因保留划分和知识图谱补全指标；3) 提出共进化离散扩散框架Method，联合建模生物上一致的离散化基因表达状态与调控关系；4) 引入TF-ALL子图采样策略实现可扩展训练。

Result: 在Benchmark上的大量实验表明，Method建立了新的最先进性能，在新型调控关系发现上显著优于现有方法；消融研究进一步验证了设计的有效性。

Conclusion: 通过重新定义任务、设计更匹配实际需求的基准和方法，本文显著提升了GRN推断在归纳场景下对高置信度新型调控关系的发现能力。

Abstract: Inferring gene regulatory networks (GRNs) from single-cell transcriptomic data is crucial for biological discovery, yet existing approaches suffer from a fundamental misalignment with real-world needs. Researchers typically seek a small set of high-confidence regulatory interactions for experimental validation, often involving previously unseen genes. However, current benchmarks rely on transductive splits with global classification metrics, while prevailing models struggle to generalize under inductive settings. To bridge this gap, we reformulate GRN inference as an inductive, ranking-centric graph completion problem and introduce \textbf{\benchmark}, a new benchmark that incorporates an inductive gene-holdout split together with knowledge graph completion metrics to better evaluate top-ranked predictions. Building on this, we propose \textbf{\method}, the first co-evolutionary discrete diffusion framework that jointly models biologically coherent discretized gene expression states and regulatory interactions for robust inductive generalization and improved top-ranked regulatory discovery. We further introduce TF-ALL Subgraph Sampling (TASS) for scalable training. Extensive experiments on {\benchmark} show that {\method} establishes new state-of-the-art performance, significantly outperforming existing methods in novel regulatory discovery, and ablation studies further verify the effectiveness of our design.

</details>


### [70] [ShortOPD: Recovering Pruned LLMs with Short-to-Long On-Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13124)
*Qingyu Zhang,Qianhao Yuan,Hongyu Lin,Yaojie Lu,Xianpei Han,Le Sun,Xiang Li,Ming Xu,Jiarui Li,Xiuyin Zhao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出短到长的策略蒸馏（ShortOPD）方法，通过检测教师模型确认的重复后缀、只保留有效前缀作为 rollout 长度，并动态分配预算，解决结构化剪枝后 LLM 在自由生成任务中性能崩溃的问题。该方法在数学、代码和开放生成任务上，将压缩模型得分提升约9倍，训练时间仅为固定长 rollout 方案的1/4，token 消耗减少71%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 结构化剪枝后的 LLM 在多选识别任务上表现尚可，但在实际部署所需的自由生成任务上性能严重下降。分析发现：1) 贪婪解码的 pass@1 几乎消失，但重复采样下 pass@k 能大幅恢复，说明有用生成被降级而非删除；2) 可恢复区域主要因后缀重复而失败。现有恢复方法（SFT、KD、SeqKD）效果有限。

Method: 提出 ShortOPD（Short-to-Long On-Policy Distillation）：1) 使用剪枝前模型作为冻结教师，对压缩模型进行策略内蒸馏；2) 检测教师模型确认的重复后缀，将其截断，只保留有效前缀作为每个 rollout 的有效长度；3) 将未来 rollout 预算分配给当前策略能处理的有效长度，实现从短到长的渐进式训练。

Result: 在数学、代码和开放生成任务上，ShortOPD 将压缩模型得分提升约9倍；相比标准恢复方法（SFT、KD、SeqKD）提升1.6-4.4倍；与固定8192 token rollout 方案相比，仅用1/4训练时间（8.5 vs 35.9小时）和71%更少的 rollout token 即可达到接近的性能（相差2个点以内）。

Conclusion: ShortOPD 有效解决了结构化剪枝 LLM 在自由生成任务中的性能恢复问题，使结构化剪枝超越仅在困惑度和多选基准上的微小改进，更接近实际部署所需的生成质量。

Abstract: Structured pruning is a hardware-friendly way to compress LLMs, but it is mostly validated on multiple-choice recognition tasks, while the same compressed checkpoints can collapse on the free-form generation that deployment actually requires. Two observations trace this gap. First, greedy \textsc{pass}@$1$ nearly vanishes after compression, yet \textsc{pass}@$k$ recovers substantially under repeated sampling: useful generations are demoted, not erased. Second, the recoverable regime fails mainly through suffix repetition. Recovery should therefore train on the compressed model's own on-policy states with dense token-level supervision, which On-Policy Distillation (OPD) provides by reusing the pre-compression model as a frozen teacher. However, long on-policy rollouts spend early recovery budget on low-information repetitive suffixes, delaying loss descent. To mitigate this waste, we propose \textbf{\shortopd}, a short-to-long OPD schedule that detects teacher-confirmed repetitive suffixes, treats the surviving prefix as each rollout's effective length, and allocates future rollout budgets to the effective lengths the policy can currently use. Across math, code, and open-ended generation, \shortopd\ raises the compressed model's score to about $9\times$ its unrecovered value and $1.6$--$4.4\times$ standard recovery recipes (SFT w/o KD, KD, and SeqKD), and it matches a fixed $8192$-token rollout horizon within two points using a quarter of the training time ($8.5$ vs.\ $35.9$ hours) and $71\%$ fewer rollout tokens. We hope this recipe helps move structured pruning beyond marginal gains on perplexity and multiple-choice benchmarks, a step closer to deployment-ready generation quality.

</details>


### [71] [HEDGEHOG: Hierarchical Evaluation of Drug Generators Through Rigorous Filtration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13155)
*Daria A. Ryabchenko,Pavel Gurevich,Shamil Kadyrov,Daria Frolova,Kseniia Fedisheva,Sergei A. Nikolenko,Alexander Shapeev,Marina A. Pak*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出HEDGEHOG基准，一个六阶段工业级过滤流程，用于评估分子生成模型的实际药物设计适用性。对23个生成器、23万分子评估显示，仅0.65%通过全部过滤，揭示现有模型生成的分子在药物化学、合成可行性、对接和三维姿态等多维度约束下表现极差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有分子生成模型评估指标不能反映生成物是否具有药物合理性和下游计算适用性，导致模型评估出现假阳性、错误假设和计算资源浪费。

Method: 设计HEDGEHOG基准，包含六个阶段：预处理、理化描述符筛选、结构警报和图完整性检查、合成可行性、对接和结合亲和力估计、三维姿态和相互作用检查。在标准化协议下评估23个分子生成器（覆盖三类模型）和23万个生成分子。

Result: 仅0.65%的初始分子通过所有六个阶段的过滤。在孤立标准下看似可接受的分子很少能同时满足药物化学、合成、对接和三维姿态约束。

Conclusion: 当前分子生成模型存在根本性局限：生成分子在多参数设计约束下的综合表现极差，亟需改进生成策略以提升实际药物发现中的可用性。

Abstract: Generative molecular models can support early drug discovery by proposing new candidate compounds de novo. In practice, useful candidates must balance target-relevant activity, synthetic accessibility, physicochemical properties, and other multiparameter design constraints. However, metrics commonly used to evaluate molecular generators only weakly reflect whether the generated compounds are medicinally plausible and suitable for downstream computation. This can produce false positives in model evaluation, incorrect assumptions, and inefficient use of computational resources. We introduce HEDGEHOG, a unified six-stage filtration benchmark that is inspired by industrial hit identification workflows: (i) preprocessing; (ii) physicochemical descriptor screening; (iii) structural alerts and graph-sanity checks; (iv) synthesis feasibility; (v) docking and binding affinity estimation; and (vi) three-dimensional pose and interaction checks. We evaluate 23 molecular generators across three model classes under a standardized protocol. Across 230,000 generated molecules, only 0.65% of initial molecules survive all stages. Our results expose a central limitation of current molecular generators: molecules that appear acceptable under isolated criteria rarely satisfy medicinal chemistry, synthesis, docking, and 3D pose filters simultaneously.

</details>


### [72] [SteinGate: Tail-Sensitive Safe Reinforcement Learning via Stein Discrepancy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13175)
*Yassine Chemingui,Chenhua Fan,Honghao Wei,Janardhan Rao Doppa*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SteinGate 提出了一种基于核化斯坦因散度的边界感知分布安全证书，通过非参数一致性检验替代传统期望成本约束，以检测和缓解强化学习中的罕见灾难性尾部事件，并在连续控制任务中显著降低训练过程中的约束违规频率与严重性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统安全强化学习依赖期望累积成本约束，无法有效检测罕见但灾难性的尾部事件，导致安全关键场景下风险控制不足。

Method: 引入 SteinGate 边界感知分布安全证书：利用核化斯坦因散度（KSD）进行非参数一致性检验，将被裁剪成本导致的边界原子纳入考量，判断策略 rollout 成本是否与安全参考分布一致。根据一致性结果动态调整学习策略——一致时优先奖励提升，偏离时切换至恢复行为。

Result: 在连续控制基准任务上，SteinGate 相较现有基线方法，在保持竞争性累计回报的同时显著降低了训练过程中约束违规的频率和严重程度。

Conclusion: 基于分布一致性检验的安全证书能够更有效地捕捉尾部风险，为安全强化学习提供一种不依赖参数化尾部拟合的鲁棒替代方案。

Abstract: Safe reinforcement learning typically enforces safety by bounding expected cumulative costs, a criterion that often fails to detect rare but catastrophic tail events. To overcome these limitations, this paper introduces SteinGate, a boundary-aware distributional safety certificate that replaces fragile tail fitting with a robust consistency check using Kernelized Stein Discrepancy while accounting for boundary atoms induced by clipped costs. SteinGate evaluates whether observed policy rollout costs remain consistent with a safe reference distribution, providing a non-parametric safety certificate. This certificate is used to dynamically adapt the learning regime: favoring reward-improving policy updates when rollouts remain consistent with the safe reference and switching to recovery behavior when the cost tail deviates. Experiments on continuous-control benchmarks demonstrate that SteinGate significantly reduces both the frequency and severity of constraint violations during training while maintaining competitive returns relative to state-of-the-art baselines.

</details>


### [73] [Concurrent Image Understanding and Generation: Self-Correcting Coupled Markov Jump Processes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13188)
*Minh-Quan Le,Armand Comas,Alexandros Lattas,Stylianos Moschoglou,Pedro Vélez,Amit Raj,Aaron Germuth,Thabo Beeler,Dimitris Samaras,Di Qiu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出自校正耦合马尔可夫跳跃过程（SC-CMJP）框架，以及无需训练的单遍采样器 CO₂Jump，实现文本与图像在生成过程中实时交叉模态校正，解决现有方法无法在同一去噪步骤内进行模态间协同校正的问题。同时发布了三个大规模联合多模态生成数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有联合多模态生成方法（如交错解码或独立并行分支更新）无法在同一去噪步骤内让一种模态感知另一种模态的最新决策，结合 MDM 无法重新掩码的特性，导致跨模态矛盾既无法检测也无法修复。

Method: 提出 SC-CMJP 框架：一种模态的转移率是另一种模态置信度得分（经跨模态注意力加权）的泛函；引入重新掩码跳跃机制，当跨模态证据不一致时立即撤回先前承诺。基于此框架设计 CO₂Jump，一种无需训练的单遍采样器。

Result: 在图像理解与编辑、视觉推理（迷宫和非诺求解）任务上取得最佳联合性能；采样器性能随去噪步数单调提升，表明跨模态耦合的收益在轨迹中持续累积。

Conclusion: 跨模态耦合的收益具有累积叠加特性，SC-CMJP 框架和 CO₂Jump 采样器为联合多模态生成提供了有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Human cognition does not separate understanding and generation. A teacher at a whiteboard speaks and draws $\textit{together}$, each modality reshapes the other. In this paper, we bring this coupled loop to artificial systems. Masked Diffusion Models (MDMs) are ideally suited to this task, yet existing samplers either decode text and image interleavedly or independently update them in parallel branches that share only previous-step history, but not the other modality's latest decisions $\textit{within}$ the same step; combined with MDMs' inability to remask, cross-modal contradictions are neither detected nor repaired. We introduce $\textbf{Self-Correcting Coupled Markov Jump Processes (SC-CMJP)}$, a framework in which one modality's transition rates are functionals of the other modality's confidence score, as weighted by cross-modal attention. Furthermore, a remasking jump retracts commitments the moment cross-modal evidence turns against them. In conjunction with SC-CMJP, we introduce $\texttt{CO}_\texttt{2}\texttt{Jump}$ (Self-$\underline{\text{CO}}$rrecting $\underline{\text{CO}}$upled $\underline{\text{Jump}}$), a novel training-free single-pass sampler for joint multimodal geneneration. For training and evaluation purposes, we have created and will release three large-scale joint multimodal generation corpora: $\text{JEdit-1M}$, $\text{JMaze-200K}$, $\text{JNono-200K}$, with matching in- and out-of-distribution benchmarks. $\texttt{CO}_\texttt{2}\texttt{Jump}$ achieves best joint performance for image understanding and editing as well as visual reasoning (maze and nonogram solving). The performance of the sampler scales monotonically with the number of denoising steps, evidence that the benefits of cross-modal coupling $\textit{compound}$ across the trajectory. Project page: https://coupled-jump.github.io

</details>


### [74] [EMAGN: Efficient Multi-Attention Graph Network via Learned Clustering for Scalable Traffic Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13241)
*Mingxing Xu,Rakesh Chowdary Machineni,Ke Liu,Xi Cheng,Chengqi Lu,Xin Hu,Lyuhao Chen,Xiangyu Li,Junwei You,Oliver Gao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种高效的多注意力图网络（EMAGN），通过将空间注意力机制线性化（基于快速高维高斯滤波理论），利用两个可学习的聚类矩阵将key和value向量分组为M个超簇，将复杂度从O(N²d)降至O(NMd)。在PEMS-BAY和METR-LA上，EMAGN在精度接近全注意力GMAN的同时，训练时间减少32%、推理时间减少38%、GPU内存减少58%，且在16注意力头下GMAN显存溢出而EMAGN仍可运行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的自注意力机制在流量预测中虽能建模动态长程依赖，但二次方的计算和内存复杂度限制了其可扩展性，亟需一种线性复杂度的注意力方法。

Method: 受快速高维高斯滤波理论启发，设计两个可学习的聚类矩阵C_k和C_v，将key和value向量自适应地分组为M个超簇，实现空间注意力的线性化，复杂度从O(N²d)降至O(NMd)。

Result: 在PEMS-BAY和METR-LA数据集上，EMAGN的MAE仅比全注意力GMAN高2.7-3.2%，但训练时间减少32%、推理时间减少38%、GPU内存减少58%；在K=16注意力头时，GMAN在11GB GPU上显存溢出，EMAGN仍可正常运行；且优于Linformer和Performer。

Conclusion: EMAGN通过交通网络感知的自适应聚类策略，在保持注意力灵活性的同时实现了线性复杂度，显著提升了可扩展性，为大规模交通预测任务提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Traffic forecasting is highly challenging due to complex and nonlinear spatial and temporal dependencies. Self-attention mechanisms have been widely adopted to model dynamic and long-range dependencies, achieving state-of-the-art performance, but suffer from limited scalability due to quadratic computational and memory complexity. To address this, we propose an Efficient Multi-Attention Graph Network (EMAGN) that linearises the spatial attention mechanism itself, inspired by the theory of fast high-dimensional Gaussian filtering. Two learned clustering matrices C_k and C_v adaptively group key and value vectors into M super-clusters, reducing complexity from O(N^2 d) to O(NMd) without sacrificing the flexibility of attention for dynamic dependency modelling. Experimental results on PEMS-BAY and METR-LA show that EMAGN achieves accuracy within 2.7-3.2% MAE of full-attention GMAN while reducing training time by 32%, inference time by 38%, and GPU memory by 58%. Critically, at K=16 attention heads, full-attention GMAN runs out of memory on a standard 11 GB GPU entirely while EMAGN continues to operate, demonstrating a categorical expansion of feasible model configurations. EMAGN also surpasses Linformer and Performer in both accuracy and efficiency within the same backbone, owing to its traffic-network-aware adaptive clustering.

</details>


### [75] [Reassessing Muon for Matrix Factorization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13246)
*Ali Parviz,Gal Mishne,Alex Cloninger*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过低秩矩阵分解这一可控问题，发现Muon优化器并不总是优于AdamW，其优势对超参数敏感，并非源于更新规则本身。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Muon在大规模深度学习中被报道优于Adam/AdamW，但其优势是来自更新规则本身还是现代深度网络的规模、架构和数据等混杂因素尚不清楚。

Method: 将Muon从现代深度网络的混杂因素中剥离，在低秩矩阵分解这一简单、理解充分且具有谱结构的问题上进行研究，并与精心调优的自适应基线进行控制性对比。

Result: Muon在低秩矩阵分解中并不始终优于AdamW，其此前被报道的若干优势对超参数选择敏感。

Conclusion: 对谱感知正交化何时有益的认知需要更加细致，并主张在端到端基准之外，也应在可控问题上评估现代优化器。

Abstract: Muon has recently emerged as a strong optimizer for large-scale deep learning, where it reshapes gradient updates through approximate orthogonalization and has been reported to outperform Adam and AdamW in large language model training. Its empirical success has motivated a growing body of theoretical work that interprets Muon as steepest descent under the spectral norm. Yet it remains unclear which of Muon's advantages stem from its update rule itself and which are artifacts of the scale, architecture, and data of modern deep networks. In this work, we isolate the optimizer from these confounding factors by studying Muon on a simple, well-understood, and spectrally structured problem: low-rank matrix factorization. Through a controlled comparison against carefully tuned adaptive baselines, we find that Muon does not consistently outperform AdamW in this setting and that several previously reported advantages are sensitive to hyperparameter choices. Our results provide a more nuanced picture of when spectrum-aware orthogonalization is beneficial and argue for evaluating modern optimizers on controlled problems in addition to end-to-end benchmarks.

</details>


### [76] [Deconstructing Actor-Critic: A Large-scale Empirical Study of Design Components for Practitioners](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13274)
*Haseeb Shah,Lingwei Zhu,Adam White,Martha White*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过超过33,000次实验，系统分析了Actor-Critic算法中各组件（策略更新方式、动作分布表示、梯度估计方法、更新频率等）对控制任务中运行变异性和超参数敏感性的影响。基于真实水处理厂的控制任务，发现高斯动作分布与路径梯度估计器等常见默认配置可靠性最差，而有界分布配合自适应更新调度在广泛设置下保持鲁棒。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习正越来越多地应用于对可靠性和调优预算有严格要求的真实世界系统（如聚变等离子体、自动驾驶、药物发现、饮用水处理），但Actor-Critic算法中设计决策对算法可靠性的影响缺乏系统理解。

Method: 基于真实水处理厂的控制任务，进行了超过33,000次实验，系统比较Actor-Critic算法中策略更新方式、动作分布表示（高斯/有界分布）、梯度估计方法（路径梯度等）、以及更新频率等组件对运行变异性和超参数敏感性的影响。

Result: 常见默认配置（如高斯动作分布+路径梯度估计器）是最不可靠的方案之一；而有界分布配合自适应更新调度在广泛设置下保持鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 为科学和工程领域的实践者提供了在将Actor-Critic方法应用于新的真实世界控制任务时，理解各组件选择并做出组件级决策的经验指导。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning is increasingly being considered for controlling real-world systems, from fusion plasma and autonomous vehicles to drug discovery and drinking water treatment, where reliability is essential and tuning budgets are limited. Actor-critic algorithms share a set of design decisions, such as how the policy is updated, how it represents the distribution over actions, how its gradient is estimated, and how often it is updated relative to the value estimator. Using a control task derived from a real water treatment plant, we analyze over 33,000 experiments to determine how these components affect variability across runs and sensitivity to hyperparameters. Common defaults, such as Gaussian action distributions with pathwise gradient estimators, are among the least reliable configurations, whereas bounded distributions with adaptive update schedules remain robust across a wide range of settings. These findings offer empirical guidance to practitioners across scientific and engineering domains for understanding and making component-level decisions when adapting actor-critic methods to new real-world control settings.

</details>


### [77] [Tabular Foundation Models for Discrete Choice Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13314)
*Liu Liu,Dan Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了如何将表格基础模型（TFMs）应用于离散选择问题。直接应用TFMs效果不佳，原因是TFM假设行独立，而离散选择具有集合依赖性和消费者异质性。作者提出了一种新的数据重构方法，编码了选择集依赖性和个体异质性。在酸奶扫描面板数据上，该方法在保留对数似然上比分层贝叶斯估计高8%，命中率高3.6%，速度快16倍，尤其在中数据量（每人10-40次购买）场景下优势最大。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索表格基础模型（TFMs）能否有效应用于离散选择这一营销与运营领域的核心需求估计框架，并解决直接应用时因结构不匹配导致的性能瓶颈。

Method: 提出一种数据重构方法，将选择集依赖性和个体异质性编码到行式学习框架中，从而使TFMs能够处理离散选择问题。

Result: 在酸奶扫描面板数据上，最优重构方法在保留对数似然上超越分层贝叶斯估计8%，命中率提升3.6%，运行速度快16倍。在中数据量（每人10-40次购买）场景下优势最大，对购买历史浅的消费者通过微调可进一步改进。

Conclusion: 建立了将基础模型应用于消费者选择问题的原则性方法，证明了TFMs通过恰当的数据重构可以有效应用于离散选择估计，尤其在中等数据规模下具有显著优势。

Abstract: Tabular foundation models (TFMs) generate predictions on structured data via in-context learning, without task-specific estimation. We ask whether TFMs can be effectively applied to discrete choice, a central demand estimation framework in marketing and operations, and find that directly applying TFMs yields limited performance. The gap is structural: TFMs assume row-independent observations, whereas discrete choice is inherently set-valued and subject to persistent consumer preference heterogeneity.
